TL;DR: 本文解决了Bures-Wasserstein（BW）重心计算的收敛性保证二分法问题：单位步长的黎曼梯度下降（RGD）在实践中快速收敛，但理论保证具有最坏情形下的指数维度依赖；而维度无关的保证又需小步长，牺牲了实际速度。作者提出投影RGD算法，在单位步长下实现维度无关的线性收敛，收敛率$(1 - κ^{-3/2})$（κ为集合条件数），相比最优小步长保证（κ^{5/2}迭代复杂度）实现了多项式改进（κ^{3/2}）。核心是新颖的投影引理：将正定矩阵特征值截断到区间[α, β]等价于BW度量下到集合{S: αI ≤ S ≤ βI}的非扩张（1-Lipschitz）闭式投影——不同于已知的单侧情形，该结论不能由凸性直接导出。该投影是"免费"的，因为它复用了下一步迭代无论如何都要执行的谱分解，因此投影与非投影迭代每步代价相同。同样的分析还覆盖了Brahmachari等人（2025）的矩阵投影问题，其不动点算法被识别为单位步长RGD在完全测地子流形上的应用，从而将该维度无关保证原样推广到该场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bures-Wasserstein（BW）重心计算在机器学习、最优传输和量子信息中广泛出现。实践中使用的单位步长RGD（不动点迭代）收敛快速，但现有理论分析存在二分法：单位步长保证携带最坏情形指数维度依赖，而维度无关保证需要小步长从而丧失实际速度。作者希望消解这一矛盾。

Method: 提出投影RGD算法：引入一个投影引理，证明将正定矩阵的特征值截断到区间[α, β]是BW度量下投影到集合{S: αI ≤ S ≤ βI}的闭式、非扩张（1-Lipschitz）投影，且不同于单侧情形，该结论不依赖于凸性。该投影免费：它复用了下一次迭代必做的特征分解，故投影与非投影迭代代价相同。分析还扩展到Brahmachari等人（2025）的矩阵投影问题，将其不动点算法识别为单位步长RGD在完全测地子流形上的情形。

Result: 投影RGD在单位步长下实现维度无关的线性收敛，收敛率$(1 - κ^{-3/2})$，其中κ为集合条件数；迭代复杂度为κ^{3/2}，多项式改进于最佳小步长保证的κ^{5/2}。该维度无关保证原样推广到Brahmachari等人的矩阵投影设置。

Conclusion: 通过投影机制解决了单位步长RGD在维度无关保证上的缺失，在不增加每步计算代价的前提下达到更优的维度无关线性收敛率，并统一推及相关的矩阵投影问题。

Abstract: The computation of the Bures-Wasserstein (BW) barycenter of an ensemble of positive definite matrices arises throughout machine learning, optimal transport, and quantum information. Riemannian gradient descent (RGD) at unit step size -- the fixed-point iteration used in practice -- converges rapidly, yet existing analyses present a dichotomy: unit-step guarantees carry worst-case exponential dependence on the dimension, while dimension-independent guarantees require small step sizes that forfeit the empirical speed. We resolve this dichotomy, not by improving the guarantees for unit-step RGD, but by proposing a Projected RGD algorithm that achieves dimension-independent linear convergence at unit step size. The achieved rate, $(1 - κ^{-3/2})$, where $κ$ is the condition number of the ensemble, also polynomially improves on the best small-step guarantee ($κ^{3/2}$ versus $κ^{5/2}$ iteration complexity). The crux is a novel Projection Lemma: clipping the eigenvalues of a positive matrix to an interval $[α, β]$ is the closed-form, non-expansive (1-Lipschitz) BW-metric projection onto the set $\{S : αI \leq S \leq βI\}$ -- a statement which, unlike its known one-sided counterpart, does not follow from convexity. The projection is moreover free: it reuses an eigendecomposition the next iteration must perform in any case, so the projected and unprojected iterations cost the same per step. The same analysis covers the invariant matrix projection problem of Brahmachari et al. (2025), whose fixed-point algorithm we identify as unit-step RGD on a totally geodesic submanifold, thereby extending the dimension-independent guarantee to that setting verbatim.

</details>


### [306] [From Nowcasting to Forecasting: Adapting a Reanalysis-Trained](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03763)
*Mikko Partio,Leila Hieta,Ossi Laine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CloudCast v2，一种用于12小时云量预报的机器学习模型。该模型先在欧洲区域再分析数据上训练以学习云演变动力学，随后利用条件流匹配这一生成方法，将噪声转化为以观测初始云场和数值天气预报输入为条件的云量预报。相比上一代CloudCast v1，它在1-12小时范围内平均绝对误差降低10%，且在约3-6小时后在分数技巧评分上超越前者，证明观测初始化的机器学习预报可突破传统1-3小时临近预报范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的云量预报对温度预测、辐射预报和太阳能发电运营至关重要。短时临近预报虽能在最初几小时保持观测到的云的位置，但当云场经历生成、消散和形变时其技巧下降；而更长时效需要考虑大气演变，但业务数值天气预报在初始化时往往无法精确再现卫星观测到的云状态。因此需要一种既能从观测初始条件出发、又能学习云演变动力学的模型。

Method: 开发CloudCast v2模型，采用两阶段训练策略：首先在哥白尼欧洲区域再分析数据上训练以学习云演变动力学，然后利用条件流匹配这一生成式方法，将模型自适应到卫星反演云场。条件流匹配将噪声转化为以观测初始云场和数值天气预报输入为条件的云量预报，从而在初始化时保留卫星云场的空间细节并同时考虑大气动力演变。

Result: 在1-12小时预报范围内，CloudCast v2相比上一代CloudCast v1将平均绝对误差降低10%；在分数技巧评分这一基于邻域的空间一致性度量上，CloudCast v2在约3-6小时后（取决于云量类别）超越CloudCast v1。

Conclusion: 结果表明，观测初始化的机器学习云量预报能够突破通常1-3小时的临近预报范围，同时保留来自卫星云场的空间细节，为更长时间尺度的云量预报提供了一种可行的方案。

Abstract: Accurate cloud-cover forecasts are important for temperature prediction, radiation forecasting, and solar-power operations. Short-range forecasting methods can preserve observed cloud placement during the first forecast hours, but their skill decreases when cloud fields evolve through formation, dissipation and deformation. Longer lead times require accounting for atmospheric evolution, but operational numerical weather prediction (NWP) forecasts may not accurately represent the satellite-observed cloud state at initialization. We develop CloudCast v2, a machine-learning model for 12-hour cloud-cover forecasting from observation-based initial conditions. The model is first trained on the Copernicus European Regional Reanalysis (Ridal2024) to learn cloud-evolution dynamics, and is then adapted to satellite-derived cloud fields using conditional flow matching (Lipman2023), a generative method that transforms noise into cloud-cover forecasts conditioned on the observed initial cloud fields and NWP inputs. CloudCast v2 reduces mean absolute error by 10% relative to its predecessor, CloudCast v1 (Partio2025), over the 1-12 h range. It also overtakes CloudCast v1 in fractions skill score, a neighborhood-based measure of spatial agreement, after approximately 3-6 h, depending on the cloudiness category. These results show that observation-initialized machine-learning forecasts can extend beyond the usual 1-3-hour nowcasting range while retaining spatial detail from satellite cloud fields.

</details>


### [307] [OBER+: Continuity-Aware Reporting and Traceable Continuous Improvement in Outcome-Based Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03770)
*Elakkiya Rajasekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 介绍OBER+系统，它将学习成果达成度的测量缺口转换为有据可依的纠正行动，并通过规则处理和验证，提高结果报告的可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于成果的教育实践虽常规计算成果达成度，但课程分析综述显示缺乏关于该计算如何指导决策的证据。

Method: 扩展现有平台OBER为OBER+，通过五个阶段：跨课程交付累计达成度、识别缺口与持续缺口、按监管机构阈值分级、记录决策并关联证据库、日志变更、量化后续缺口变化，并添加规则比较成果陈述的连续性。

Result: 应用于两门真实课程：核心课程所有成果连续交付间被实质性重定义，导致朴素解读出现25点虚假塌陷；六项计算与文档规则不符，识别出缺陷；五对成果陈述完全相同，其余六对出现编号错位。

Conclusion: 贡献是基于规则的计算设计，任何成果平台都可实现，并提供在真实机构记录中可见的证据。

Abstract: Institutions practising outcome-based education compute learning outcome attainment routinely, while reviews of curriculum analytics report an absence of evidence on how that computation informs decisions. This paper presents OBER+, an extension of a deployed institutional attainment platform that computes the step from a measured shortfall to an evaluated corrective action. Five connected stages accumulate attainment across deliveries of a course, signal a shortfall and a persistent shortfall, grade it on cutoffs the regulator already uses, record the decision against a catalogue of practices annotated with their evidence, log the change, and quantify the subsequent movement in the shortfall. A further rule compares successive statements of an outcome, so attainment is never read as a series across a point at which the outcome changed. Applying the rules to the live record of two real courses produced three results. Every outcome of a core course was substantively redefined between consecutive deliveries, with subject matter moving between outcome numbers, so a naive reading would have reported a twenty-five point collapse between quantities that do not refer to the same learning. Recomputing the platform's figures from its documented rule showed six of ten differing by more than rounding explains, in a pattern that identified a defect since reported to the institution. Across fifteen statement pairs from three transitions, five were identical character for character, and among the ten that were not, the outcome carrying a given number was nearest to a differently numbered earlier outcome in six, a result resting on an ordering of similarities and requiring no threshold and no labelling. The contribution is a computational design for outcome-based reporting, stated as rules any attainment platform can implement, with evidence of what they make visible in a live institutional record.

</details>


### [308] [Landmark-Based Discrimination of Injury-Associated Athlete-Sessions from Minute-Resolution Multimodal Football Monitoring Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03790)
*Evangelos Chatzidimitriou,Konstantinos Tserpes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种固定里程碑、每个运动员-会话一个表示（one-representation-per-athlete-session）的建模框架，用以解决运动员监测数据（逐分钟记录）与伤病标签（仅会话级）之间的时间粒度不匹配问题。基于2020 SoccerMon数据集（48名女子精英足球运动员、3,743个运动员-会话、22个伤病相关会话）进行验证，主要评估CUM+DYN组合表示的Logistic Regression模型，其ROC-AUC介于0.367-0.607、PR-AUC介于0.0080-0.0150，结果存在较大不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动员监测数据以分钟级记录，而伤病信息仅保留会话级标签（整个会话是否与伤病相关），直接把会话标签赋给每一分钟会隐含假设“伤病的精确发生时刻已知”，这在实际中是不支持的。作者旨在回应这种数据粒度不匹配导致的建模问题，避免产生不切实际的分钟级伤病监督。

Method: 提出固定里程碑（landmark）框架：在每个里程碑（如同一会话内的第10、20、30分钟）为每个运动员-会话构建一个表示，该表示仅利用截至该时点观测到的信息；目标仍保持为会话级（整个会话是否与伤病相关），从而规避分钟级标签。在每一里程碑评估判别能力如何随更多会话内信息累积而改变。实验采用运动员不相交验证、运动员簇自助抽样不确定性、共同队列敏感性分析、替代阴性运动员折分配、等权重运动员加权，并与Logistic Regression、Random Forest、XGBoost基准比较，比较会话前（PRE）、累积（CUM）、动态（DYN）及组合表示。

Result: 在3,743个运动员-会话（含来自5名运动员的22个伤病相关会话）上，主要CUM+DYN Logistic Regression在各个里程碑的ROC-AUC为0.367-0.607，PR-AUC为0.0080-0.0150，且不确定性区间较宽。包含PRE的表示在若干里程碑上点估计更高，但结果仍不稳定、存在较大不确定性。

Conclusion: 该固定里程碑的会话级表示框架能有效缓解监测数据与伤病标签的时间粒度不匹配问题；但当前模型判别性能有限且存在明显不确定性，说明单纯依赖该数据及表示方式进行会话级伤病预测仍有较大局限，需要更谨慎地解释结果并考虑改进策略。

Abstract: Athlete monitoring data may be recorded minute by minute throughout a match or training session, while injury information may only indicate whether the entire session was injury-associated.
  This creates a modelling problem: assigning the same session-level label to every minute would imply that injury status is known at each exact time, even though within-session injury onset is unknown.
  Our novelty is a fixed-landmark, one-representation-per-athlete-session formulation that directly addresses this mismatch. Instead of labelling every minute, we construct one representation per athlete-session at each landmark using information observed up to that point. This keeps the target at the session level and avoids unsupported minute-level injury supervision.
  A landmark is a fixed time point within the same session, such as 10, 20, or 30 minutes. At each landmark, we assess whether the whole session is injury-associated or non-injury-associated and examine how discrimination changes as more within-session information becomes available.
  Using 2020 SoccerMon data, we analyse 3,743 athlete-sessions from 48 elite women's football athletes, including 22 injury-associated sessions from five athletes. We evaluate pre-session, cumulative, dynamic, and combined representations with athlete-disjoint validation, athlete-cluster bootstrap uncertainty, common-cohort sensitivity analysis, alternative negative-athlete fold allocations, equal-athlete weighting, and Logistic Regression, Random Forest, and XGBoost benchmarks.
  Primary CUM+DYN Logistic Regression yields ROC-AUC 0.367-0.607 and PR-AUC 0.0080-0.0150 across landmarks, with wide uncertainty. PRE-containing representations show higher point estimates at several landmarks but remain uncertain.

</details>


### [309] [From Ordered Bernoulli Levels to Critical-Line Geometry: Integer Quantization, Bernoulli Residual Phase, and Prime-Power Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03801)
*Y. Kenan Yılmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有序Bernoulli词核 f(p,n,k)=p^k(1-p)^(n-k) 及其整数倒数水平集的几何结构。通过共轭对称的复延拓，p=1/2成为唯一实分裂锚点，并产生关于实数轴的垂直几何；二次坐标Q(z)=z(1-z)在中心点处有尖锐最小值并允许精确整数量化。对于临界线零点纵坐标gamma_k，诱导水平L_k=1/4+gamma_k^2可精确分解为最近整数N_k与周期性的第一Bernoulli残差delta_k之和；圆化变换Z_k=exp(2πi delta_k)将gamma_k^2模1作为残差相位变量。唯一分解将整数壳层化为素生成元坐标，而另一复指数s将该构造提升至Dirichlet原子m^(-s)，从而联结Dirichlet级数与Euler积。文中明确区分了精确恒等式、经典zeta关联、数值检验与开放的条件Weyl测试，且不声称证明了黎曼猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者尝试从组合概率核（Bernoulli词）的几何结构出发，考察其水平集与临界线零点纵坐标之间的联系，试图揭示gamma_k^2模1的相位结构，并建立Dirichlet级数与Euler积之间的统一视角。其动机在于不依赖zeta函数的先验输入，而从基础几何与整数分解出发构造新的表示。

Method: 方法包括：1) 对核进行补集保持的复延拓，形成共轭对称的垂直几何；2) 引入二次坐标Q(z)=z(1-z)并给出整数量化；3) 将L_k=1/4+gamma_k^2分解为最近整数N_k与第一Bernoulli残差delta_k；4) 通过圆化Z_k=exp(2πi delta_k)将gamma_k^2模1隔离为相位变量；5) 利用唯一分解将整数壳层表示为素生成元坐标；6) 用复指数s推广到Dirichlet原子m^(-s)，从而连接Dirichlet级数与Euler积。

Result: 得到一组精确恒等式：水平集在p=1/2处具有唯一实分裂锚点，复延拓生成共轭对称垂直几何；二次坐标在中心点取最小值并实现整数量化；临界零点纵坐标的平方模1由周期第一Bernoulli残差delta_k经圆化后完全刻画；整数壳层通过唯一分解为素生成元坐标表示；同一构造经复指数s提升后同时解释Dirichlet级数与Euler积两套装配。数值控制和开放的条件Weyl测试被明确区分。

Conclusion: 论文并未声称证明黎曼猜想，而是建立了一套连接Bernoulli组合核、临界零点纵坐标模1结构、整数分解与Dirichlet/Euler积的几何框架。其中精确的部分与经典的zeta关联、数值检验、开放条件测试被严格分离，为后续有条件验证或进一步研究提供了结构化的基础。

Abstract: We study the ordered Bernoulli-word kernel f(p,n,k)=p^k(1-p)^(n-k) and the geometry generated by its inverse-integer level sets. The binary level 2^(-n) selects p=1/2 as the unique real split-independent anchor. Under complement-preserving complex continuation, the pair becomes z=1/2+iu and 1-z=1/2-iu, producing a conjugation-symmetric vertical geometry before any zeta-function input is introduced. The quadratic coordinate Q(z)=z(1-z)=1/4+u^2 has a sharp minimum at the central point and admits an exact integer quantization. For critical-line zero ordinates gamma_k, the induced levels L_k=1/4+gamma_k^2 are decomposed exactly as L_k=N_k+delta_k, where N_k is the nearest integer and delta_k is a periodic first-Bernoulli residual. Circularization gives Z_k=exp(2 pi i delta_k), isolating gamma_k^2 mod 1 as the residual phase variable. Unique factorization resolves the integer shells into prime-generator coordinates, while a distinct complex exponent s lifts the same construction to the Dirichlet atoms m^(-s), linking the Dirichlet-series and Euler-product assemblies. Exact identities, classical zeta connections, numerical controls, and open conditional Weyl tests are kept explicitly separate. No proof of the Riemann Hypothesis is claimed.

</details>


### [310] [Free Pause Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03807)
*John Langford,Nathan Godey,Giovanni Monea,Yoav Artzi,Harry Dong,Ying Fan,Gustavo de Rosa,Zheng Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"自由暂停令牌"（free pause token），在权重共享的骨干网络上以并行预测流的形式为语言模型提供额外的计算，而非在序列中追加令牌。在1B参数模型上，其将下一个令牌预测的困惑度（以centinats计）提升了2-3，同时增加很少上下文长度、KV缓存与推理延迟，训练算力开销最低仅为标准预训练流程的约1.14倍，实现相对于标准下一令牌训练Transformer在等算力、等参数、等令牌数下的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暂停/思考令牌通过额外序列令牌为模型提供更多计算，但会引入上下文长度、KV缓存与推理延迟等成本。作者希望获得类似额外计算收益的同时消除这些推理代价，实现等算力、等参数和等令牌条件下的性能提升。

Method: 在权重共享骨干网络基础上，引入一个"自由暂停令牌"：它不占用序列中的位置，而是作为并行预测流在每个现有位置的预测中携带额外计算。该机制通过训练阶段增加的计算换取推理阶段无额外上下文、无额外KV缓存且基本无延迟的收益，并研究多种训练管线以降低额外训练开销。

Result: 在1B参数模型上，相比标准训练，该方法将下一个令牌预测性能提升2-3 centinats；推理时几乎不增加上下文长度、KV缓存与延迟；训练算力开销在优化管线后可降到标准预训练流程的约1.14倍，同时保留大部分收益。

Conclusion: 所提方法是标准下一令牌训练Transformer在等算力、等参数、等令牌条件下的改进，说明无需在序列中添加令牌即可获得"思考"式额外计算带来的收益，推理端成本可忽略，主要代价仅在训练端且可有效控制。

Abstract: A free pause token gives a language model extra compute to form each next-token prediction (as a pause, or thinking, token does) but carries that compute in a parallel prediction stream over a weight-shared backbone rather than as an extra token in the sequence. It improves next-token prediction by 2-3 centinats in practice on a 1B parameter model. Because the pause rides an existing position instead of adding one, it is free to use: at inference it adds no context length, no KV cache, and essentially no latency with the growth in inference flops typically irrelevant as it is not the active bottleneck on throughput. The only primary cost is in training, where additional training compute versus an optimized pretraining pipeline is reduced to as low as x1.14 while preserving most of the benefits. The result is an isoflop, isoparameter, and isotoken improvement over standard next token trained transformers.

</details>


### [311] [A Peer-Relative Representation Learning Framework for Energy Inefficiency Identification in Mobile Network Sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03809)
*Eliud Nyakweba Koto,Jaco du Toit,Adham Stoltz,Johan du Preez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督的同类相对（peer-relative）方法，用于检测移动网络站点级别的能源低效问题。其核心创新是一种引入基于能量的排斥机制的能源感知最小失真嵌入（MDE）公式，使能源消耗异常偏高的站点在嵌入空间中被推离本地邻域，从而通过同类对比识别潜在低效站点并生成异常评分，为运营商优先安排现场排查提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源消耗是移动网络运营商最大的运营开支之一，但站点层面的能源低效（如故障冷却控制器、空闲无线设备、寄生辅助负荷）常因缺乏真实标签而难以被发现，历史测量数据本身可能已包含嵌入式的低效信息，因此需要一种不依赖标签的检测方法。

Method: 基于"结构相似和运营特征相似的站点应具有可比能耗"的前提，扩展标准MDE目标，加入基于能量的排斥机制（energy-based repulsion），使相对于同类站点能源消耗异常偏高的站点在低维嵌入空间中被推离其局部邻域。所得低维表示同时保留结构相似性并编码能耗偏差，通过同类相对比较识别潜在低效站点并推导异常评分。

Result: 实验结果证明，所提方法在检测性能上优于传统异常检测基线，并为移动网络的大规模能效优化提供了稳健基础。

Conclusion: 该方法为移动网络运营商提供了一种无需标注即可优先安排现场调查的实用机制，可将工程资源聚焦于最可能带来节能收益的站点，支持大规模能效优化。

Abstract: Energy consumption is one of the largest operational expenditure items for mobile network operators, yet site-level energy inefficiencies such as faulty cooling controllers, idle radio equipment, and parasitic auxiliary loads often remain undetected because no ground-truth inefficiency labels exist and historical measurements may already contain embedded inefficiencies. This study proposes an unsupervised peer-relative approach based on the premise that sites with similar structural and operational characteristics should exhibit comparable energy consumption. To capture these relationships, a novel energy-aware Minimum Distortion Embedding (MDE) formulation is introduced that extends the standard MDE objective with an energy-based repulsion mechanism. This encourages sites with anomalously high energy consumption relative to comparable peers to become displaced from their local neighbourhoods in the embedding space. The resulting low-dimensional representation simultaneously preserves structural similarity and encodes energy-related deviations, enabling the identification of potentially inefficient sites through peer-relative comparison. The derived anomaly scores provide a practical mechanism for prioritising field investigations, allowing mobile network operators to focus engineering resources on sites most likely to yield energy savings. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms conventional anomaly detection baselines and provides a robust foundation for large-scale energy-efficiency optimisation in mobile networks.

</details>


### [312] [Witnesses Explain Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03826)
*Lamine Diop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WAND是一种"设计上即可解释"的无监督表格异常检测器，它围绕单位球面上的方向组织计算，通过投影是否逃逸出次高斯极值基线来打分；其"见证方向"（witness directions）既是异常打分的依据，也是天然的特征级解释，无需额外后处理即可获得，且与SHAP/LIME等事后解释方法相比更精确、更忠实、查询成本更低。在47个ADBench数据集上与16个无监督基线对比，WAND在ROC-AUC持平的同时取得最优平均Friedman排名，以零精度代价换来了可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流无监督异常检测器虽能高效地对未标注、含污染的样本逐点打分，却无法说明为何标记某点；可解释性日益成为刚需。当前普遍的解决方案是事后使用SHAP或LIME对检测器进行近似解释，这需要对每个点反复查询检测器数千次，既昂贵又只是近似逼近，缺乏原生的、与打分一致的归因机制。

Method: WAND将计算组织在单位球面上的方向之上：对每个点，按其投影逃逸出次高斯极值基线的程度进行打分。其独创性在于，用于标记该点的"见证方向"本身就是特征空间中的向量，从而天然构成逐特征归因解释——该解释在打分的同时零成本获得，且因打分函数可微，还能通过梯度恢复。打分复杂度与样本规模线性相关，且探针效率界保证每个异常点必有见证方向（即必有解释）。

Result: 在47个ADBench数据集上，WAND在与16个无监督基线ROC-AUC持平的前提下取得了最优平均Friedman排名；其原生解释相比事后方法SHAP/LIME以及ECOD更准确、更忠实，而查询开销仅为它们的一小部分。

Conclusion: WAND是一种实用且可解释的可解释异常检测方案：它在不损失检测精度的前提下，以设计内生的方式提供免费、准确、忠实的特征级解释，从而兼顾了异常检测的性能与可解释性需求。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection scores each point of an unlabelled, contaminated sample in a single pass, and increasingly must also explain why a point is flagged. Yet the dominant detectors give a score with no account of which features drive it, and explanations are bolted on post-hoc with SHAP or LIME, which re-query the detector thousands of times per point and only approximate it. We introduce WAND, an unsupervised tabular anomaly detector that is explainable by design. WAND organises its computation around directions on the unit sphere, scoring each point by how far its projection escapes a sub-Gaussian extreme-value baseline. The originality of our approach is that the witness directions that flag a point, being vectors in feature space, are its explanation, a per-feature attribution obtained at no cost over scoring and, since the score is differentiable, recoverable by gradients. Scoring is linear in the sample size, and a probe-efficiency bound guarantees every anomaly a witness, hence an explanation. Across 47 ADBench datasets WAND attains the best mean Friedman rank at ROC-AUC parity with 16 unsupervised baselines, so the gain is interpretability at no accuracy cost; its native explanations are more accurate and faithful than post-hoc SHAP/LIME and ECOD at a fraction of the query cost. WAND is thus a practical, interpretable solution for explainable anomaly detection.

</details>


### [313] [Multi-step Proximal Policy Improvement in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03842)
*Soohyun Choi,Seonvin Cho,Songnam Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种离线强化学习（Offline RL）的策略更新几何视角：将策略建模为带有度量几何的概率流形，将广泛的离线actor目标统一解释为单步近端策略改进（SPI）。在此基础上提出多步近端策略改进（MPI）机制，通过串联重定心的近端步骤，在保持近端控制的同时实现超越数据集支持范围的受控策略改进，并在D4RL基准上提升了TD3+BC、ReBRAC、IQL等强基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习必须权衡两个竞争需求：策略更新应接近数据集支持的动作以保持价值估计可靠，但有效增益往往需要超越行为分布。现有方法难以在保持近端控制的同时实现有意义的策略改进，作者希望从几何角度统一理解并解决这一矛盾。

Method: 将策略视为具有特定度量几何的概率流形，将广泛的离线actor目标统一解释为单步近端策略改进（SPI），即critic定义能量的流形梯度流的隐式离散化。在此基础上提出多步近端策略改进（MPI），通过串联顺序重定心的近端步骤实现受控改进。框架支持多种策略几何，并给出了确定性和对角高斯策略的实用实例。

Result: 在D4RL基准上，少量MPI细化即可提升TD3+BC、ReBRAC和IQL等强离线基线在多项任务上的表现。定向诊断实验进一步区分了重定心细化与固定目标更新调度的差异，并刻画了critic误差下的局限性。

Conclusion: MPI作为一种即插即用的细化机制，能在保持近端控制的同时实现受控的离线策略改进，为离线RL策略更新提供了统一的理论框架和有效的实践方法，但同时需要关注其受critic误差影响的局限。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) must reconcile two competing requirements: policy updates should stay near dataset-supported actions to keep value estimates reliable, yet meaningful gains often require moving beyond the behavior distribution. We develop a geometric view of offline actor updates by modeling policies as a probability manifold endowed with a chosen metric geometry. Under this lens, a broad class of offline actor objectives can be interpreted as a single proximal policy improvement step (SPI), i.e., an implicit discretization of a manifold gradient flow induced by a critic-defined energy. Building on this insight, we propose multi-step proximal policy improvement (MPI), a plug-in refinement mechanism that composes sequential re-centered proximal steps. MPI enables controlled policy improvement beyond dataset support while retaining proximal control at each refinement. The framework accommodates multiple policy geometries and admits practical instantiations for deterministic and diagonal-Gaussian policies. Experiments on D4RL benchmarks show that small numbers of MPI refinements improve strong offline baselines, including TD3+BC, ReBRAC, and IQL, on many tasks. Focused diagnostics further distinguish re-centered refinement from fixed-objective update scheduling and characterize limitations under critic error.

</details>


### [314] [Pushing the (Decision) Boundaries: Dynamically Calibrating Differentially Private Noise to Explainability in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03851)
*Michael Khavkin,Kichang Lee,Jaeho Jin,JeongGil Ko,Eran Toch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 XCal-FL，一种用于跨站点联邦学习的可解释性驱动的闭环本地训练算法。它利用三种互补信号（预测logit变化、反事实边界、显著性集中度）动态校准差分隐私（DP）噪声，并借助自适应隐私核算保证形式化DP保障。在三个医学影像数据集上的实验表明，相比静态噪声FL，XCal-FL将预测性能提升超10%、解释保真度提升最高5倍，且隐私预算效率更高；分析还揭示解释保真度与预测性能在隐私损失维度上呈现不同的非线性动态，说明可解释性是隐私权衡中不可由效用推断的独立维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带差分隐私的联邦学习虽广泛用于保护分布式机器学习中的数据机密性，但DP噪声会扭曲所学表示并降低解释保真度，这在需要可信解释的场景（如辅助临床诊断）中构成瓶颈。先前工作仅以静态特征重要性信号适配DP噪声，将可解释性局限于事后分析，无法在训练过程中依据解释质量对噪声进行校准。因此需要一种能在训练期间动态调节DP噪声以同时优化准确性与可解释性的方法。

Method: 提出XCal-FL，一种可解释性驱动的闭环本地训练算法。核心是从三个互补信号动态校准DP噪声：(1) 预测logit变化，衡量对模型置信度的因果影响；(2) 反事实边界，捕获决策边界敏感性；(3) 显著性集中度，量化模型注意力的空间一致性。同时通过自适应隐私核算（adaptive privacy accounting）保证形式化的DP保障，形成训练中噪声自我调节的闭环机制。

Result: 在三个医学影像数据集、多种FL配置下的实验表明：XCal-FL生成的全局模型更准确且更可解释，相比静态噪声FL，预测性能提升超10%、解释保真度最高提升5倍，并在保真度上优于最先进的自适应DP方法。此外XCal-FL隐私预算效率更高，每单位累积隐私损失带来准确性和解释保真度的更大提升。进一步分析发现，预测性能随隐私损失近似线性增长，而解释保真度呈现非线性动态。

Conclusion: 研究发现可解释性是隐私权衡中独立于效用的一个维度，无法仅从实用指标推断。这一结论对决策关键型应用中训练与隐私预算分配具有重要启示，表明需要在可解释性与隐私之间建立专门的优化与评估框架，而非单纯依靠准确性权衡。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) with Differential Privacy (DP) is increasingly adopted to preserve data confidentiality in distributed machine learning. However, DP noise distorts learned representations and degrades explanation fidelity, limiting differentially private FL where trustworthy explanations are required, such as assistive clinical diagnosis. Prior work adapted DP noise with static feature-importance signals, restricting explainability to post hoc analysis and precluding noise calibration to explanation quality during training. We propose XCal-FL, a closed-loop, explainability-driven local training algorithm for image classification in cross-silo FL that dynamically calibrates DP noise from three complementary signals: (1) prediction logit variations, measuring causal influence on model confidence, (2) counterfactual margins, capturing decision-boundary sensitivity, and (3) saliency concentration, quantifying spatial coherence of model attention, while enforcing formal DP guarantees via adaptive privacy accounting. Experiments on three medical imaging datasets across varying FL configurations show that XCal-FL yields more accurate and interpretable global models, improving predictive performance by over 10\% and explanation fidelity by up to 5$\times$ over static-noise FL, and outperforming state-of-the-art adaptive DP methods in fidelity. XCal-FL also achieves higher privacy-budget efficiency, turning each unit of cumulative privacy loss into larger gains in both accuracy and explanation fidelity. Our analysis further reveals that, unlike predictive performance, which scales roughly linearly with privacy loss, explanation fidelity exhibits non-linear dynamics. These findings suggest explainability is a distinct dimension of the privacy trade-off that cannot be inferred from utility alone, with implications for training and privacy-budget allocation in decision-critical applications.

</details>


### [315] [High-Dimensional Learning Dynamics of Attention-Indexed Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03858)
*Yizhou Xu,Margarita Sagitova,Lenka Zdeborová,Florent Krzakala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究注意力机制的训练动力学，尤其关注注意力矩阵具有大秩的情况。在合适的高维极限下，总体损失景观由有限个迹序参数刻画；而在线随机梯度下降则由无穷矩阵矩层级支配，但可被有限截断系统指数级近似。研究发现注意力参数化本身是一种架构隐式偏差：直接优化注意力矩阵S可能陷入无信息状态，绑定形式S=WW^T自动引发对称破缺并以Θ(d²log d)样本实现弱恢复，非绑定形式S=UV^T则呈现快慢机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制是现代基础模型的核心，但其训练动力学（尤其是注意力矩阵具有高秩时的行为）尚未被充分理解。作者旨在建立统一的注意力索引模型框架，从理论上揭示注意力参数化对训练的影响（隐式偏差），并解释不同参数化下弱恢复的样本复杂度差异。

Method: 提出注意力索引模型这一广泛框架，可表示多层和多头注意力架构。在高维极限下分析总体损失景观的序参数结构（有限个迹序参数）；分析在线SGD的矩阵矩层级演化，并证明其可被有限截断系统指数近似。通过比较直接参数化S、绑定参数化S=WW^T与非绑定参数化S=UV^T的训练动力学，刻画各自的隐式偏差和快慢时间尺度机制。

Result: 总体损失的景观由有限迹序参数刻画，而SGD由无穷矩阵矩层级支配但可指数近似截断。直接优化S会陷入无信息状态；绑定形式S=WW^T自动产生对称破缺，实现Θ(d²log d)样本的弱恢复；非绑定形式S=UV^T存在快慢机制，预激活均值先在快时间尺度演化、重叠矩阵在慢时间尺度演化，当快动力学所选态打破初始对称性时，在Θ(d²log d)尺度发生弱恢复。

Conclusion: 注意力参数化本身构成一种架构层面的隐式偏差，绑定与非绑定形式的训练动力学差异（对称破缺/快慢机制）决定了弱恢复性能。该框架为理解注意力机制训练动力学与隐式偏差提供了统一理论视角，并揭示了参数化选择对学习能力的关键影响。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern foundation models, yet their training dynamics remain poorly understood, especially when the attention matrices have extensive rank. In this work, we study attention-indexed models, a broad framework that can represent multi-layer and multi-head attention architectures. First, we show that, in a suitable high-dimensional limit, the population-loss landscape is characterized by a finite set of trace order parameters. In contrast, online stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is governed by an infinite hierarchy of matrix moments, which we show can be exponentially well-approximated by a finite truncated system. Second, this framework reveals that attention parameterization itself can act as an architectural implicit bias. Direct optimization of an attention matrix $S\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}$ can remain trapped in an uninformative state. Tied attention ($S=WW^\top$) induces an automatic symmetry-breaking mechanism and yields weak recovery in $Θ(d^2\log d)$ samples. For untied attention, $S=UV^\top$, we uncover a fast-slow mechanism: the pre-activation mean first evolves on a fast timescale, while the overlaps evolve on a slower one. Weak recovery on the $Θ(d^2\log d)$ scale occurs when the state selected by the fast dynamics breaks the initial symmetry.

</details>


### [316] [Differentiable Interval Bottlenecks for Interpretable Anomaly Detection in Numerical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03878)
*Lamine Diop,Marc Plantevit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DIFFINT是一种可解释的基于重构的异常检测自编码器，其潜在瓶颈被结构化为一组端到端学习的软、轴对齐区间隶属度（hyper-rectangle），无需离散化或二值化。每个潜在单元对应特征空间中的可读超矩形，实例按落入各区间的强度进行编码，重构误差作为异常分数。该方法在48个ADBench基准上对22个基线取得了最佳平均排名（ROC-AUC 4.10，AUPR 4.16），且是统计并列领先簇中唯一的可解释检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于重构的异常检测器（如深度自编码器）准确但缺乏可解释性——它们能标记异常样本，却无法告知从业者具体哪些特征范围导致异常。作者旨在保留差分表示学习的强大能力的同时，暴露可检查的内部结构，使异常检测既准确又可审计。

Method: DIFFINT将自编码器的潜在瓶颈设计为一组软性、轴对齐的区间隶属度，直接从原始数值数据端到端学习，无需预处理离散化或二值化。每个潜在单元对应特征空间中的一个可读超矩形；实例以相对于其他单元的落入强度进行编码，重构误差作为异常分数。方法包含三点技术支撑：(1) 精确的归纳偏置——通过Lipschitz约束的解码器，为落在学习支持集所有有效坐标之外的点提供经认证的重构误差下界；(2) 分级、经验验证的抑制机制，处理仅少数特征异常的常见情形；(3) 闭式、无标签的重要性指标，从模型已维持的量中为每个(单元,特征)对排序，将训练好的区间转化为可审计的候选约束，无需任何异常标签。

Result: 在48个ADBench基准上对22个基线、统一[-1,1]归一化协议下，DIFFINT在ROC-AUC（4.10）和AUPR（4.16）两个指标上均取得最佳平均排名；在仅用正常样本（inlier-only）检测器中明显领先其类别，并与最强的含污染数据检测器竞争相当（见分层与完整案例分析）。它是统计并列领先簇（七个方法）中唯一的可解释检测器。

Conclusion: DIFFINT表明，将潜在瓶颈结构化为可读的轴对齐区间，可以在不牺牲检测性能的前提下实现可解释的异常检测。它提供了认证的重构误差下界、分级异常抑制机制以及无标签重要性排序，将训练好的区间转化为可审计的候选约束，成为既保持深度表示学习效果又具备可检查内部结构的综合方案，并具备与最先进检测器竞争的性能。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based anomaly detectors are accurate but opaque: a deep autoencoder flags a sample without telling a practitioner which feature ranges made it anomalous. We propose DIFFINT, an autoencoder whose latent bottleneck is structured as a set of soft, axis-aligned interval memberships learned end-to-end directly from raw numerical data, without any discretization or binarization. Each latent unit corresponds to a human-readable hyper-rectangle in feature space; an instance is encoded by how strongly it falls inside each interval relative to the other units, and its reconstruction error is the anomaly score. This keeps the power of differentiable representation learning while exposing an inspectable internal structure. We make the inductive bias precise: a certified reconstruction-error lower bound for points that fall outside every active coordinate of the learned support (with a Lipschitz-enforced decoder), and a graded, empirically verified suppression mechanism for the usual case in which only a few features are abnormal; and we provide a closed-form, label-free importance that ranks each (unit, feature) pair from quantities the model already maintains, turning trained intervals into auditable candidate constraints without ever seeing an anomaly label. On 48 ADBench benchmarks against 22 baselines under a common [-1, 1]-normalized protocol, DIFFINT attains the best mean rank overall on both metrics (4.10 on ROC-AUC, 4.16 on AUPR); among inlier-only detectors it leads its regime clearly, and it is competitive with the strongest contaminated-data detectors (see the stratified and complete-case analyses). It is the only interpretable detector in the statistically-tied leading cluster of seven methods.

</details>


### [317] [Beyond Endpoint Scores: Time- and Capacity-Conditioned Evaluation of Continual Knowledge Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03900)
*Heejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出，持续知识更新方法常仅依据最终检查点和一个传统adapter秩来评判优劣，这不足以确定更优的工作点。作者在固定周期性层级结构的同时，用累积回放（replay）在24个月Wikidata流上进行对比实验，并变换评估月份、回放LoRA秩和查询表述，发现“获胜者”会随这些因素变化。基于此，作者建议报告轨迹与容量扫描，仅在评估区域内排序稳定时才能宣告稳健的获胜者；否则应报告获胜区域与保留-稳定性-成本前沿。在该协议下，周期性层级只是更新成本更低的工作点，而非质量上的获胜者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续知识更新的评测过于简化：仅用单个最终检查点与单一传统adapter秩比较，无法真正判断方法的优劣。作者希望揭示评测时间点与回放侧容量这两个被忽视的因素会显著改变方法排名，从而推动更严谨的评测协议。

Method: 在固定周期性层级结构的前提下，将其与累积回放（cumulative replay）在24个月Wikidata数据流上进行比较。实验同时变化三个维度：评估月份（评测时间点）、回放LoRA秩（容量）、查询表述（query formulation）。两个基准模型Qwen2.5-1.5B与Llama-3.2-1B，并包含留出（held-out）改写句验证。

Result: 出现明显的排名反转：在Qwen2.5-1.5B上，周期性层级相对秩-8回放有5.0分优势，但对秩-72回放却变为11.6分劣势；在高秩时，若只看对齐的最终检查点可能显示打平，而时间平均回放则领先9–13分。同样的随秩反转现象在Llama-3.2-1B及留出改写句上也成立。

Conclusion: 持续更新中的方法排名同时依赖于“何时测量性能”与“回放侧获得多少自适应容量”。因此应报告评估轨迹与容量扫描，并仅在排序跨评估区域稳定时才宣称稳健获胜者；否则应报告获胜区域和保留-稳定性-成本前沿。在该协议下，周期性层级被定位为更新成本更低的工作点，而非质量上的胜者。

Abstract: Continual knowledge-updating methods are often declared superior from one final checkpoint and one conventional adapter rank. We show that this can be insufficient to identify the better operating point. Holding a periodic hierarchy fixed, we compare it with cumulative replay over a 24-month Wikidata stream while varying evaluation month, replay LoRA rank, and query formulation. The apparent winner changes across this region: on Qwen2.5-1.5B, the hierarchy's 5.0-point advantage over rank-8 replay becomes an 11.6-point deficit against rank-72 replay, and at high ranks a consolidation-aligned endpoint can suggest a tie while time-averaged replay leads by 9-13 points. The same rank-conditioned reversal appears on Llama-3.2-1B and held-out paraphrases.
  These results show that method ranking in continual updating can depend jointly on when performance is measured and how much replay-side adaptation capacity the baseline receives. We therefore propose reporting trajectories and capacity sweeps, and declaring a robust winner only when the ordering is stable across the evaluation region; otherwise, comparisons should report winner regions and retention-stability-cost frontiers. Under this protocol, the periodic hierarchy is a lower-update-cost operating point, not a quality winner.

</details>


### [318] [RATL: Learning from Retrieved Residuals for Robust Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03937)
*Yuchen He,Yueyang Cang,Zhiyuan Ning,Ningyu Wang,Li Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RATL（残差检索与反馈校正）方法，将检索增强生成（RAG）的思想从参数模型扩展到连续输出回归，用于多变量时间序列预测。RATL冻结基础预测器构建检索键，将历史预测残差转化为仅训练可用的记忆库，推理时根据因果可用性约束检索相似历史上下文中的残差轨迹，并通过面向预测块和变量的集合感知路由器选择与组合这些轨迹，实现冻结基础预测器的即插式性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG的思想（用外部证据补充参数模型）对连续输出回归同样具有吸引力，但直接复用检索到的目标值在样本之间存在输出水平、数值尺度或局部动力学差异时往往不够稳健。此外，传统预测流程一般只用残差做模型优化和误差诊断，并不把单个历史残差样本作为推理时可访问的记忆保留下来。因此需要一种能够将历史预测误差作为可检索记忆用于推理反馈校正的新范式。

Method: RATL冻结基础预测器（primary为iTransformer）来构造检索键（keys），并将该模型的历史预测残差转化为与基础模型绑定、仅训练用的记忆库。推理时，RATL在因果可用性约束下从相似的历史上下文中检索残差轨迹；随后使用一个面向预测块（forecast blocks）和变量（variables）的集合感知路由器（set-aware router）来选择并组合这些残差轨迹。训练阶段还涉及验证集上的校正强度选择，以避免残差过度注入。

Result: 实验表明，与当前上下文匹配的历史残差包含可复用的预测信息；RATL在大多数实验设置中提升了冻结基础预测器的性能。消融实验显示，学习得到的路由（learned routing）能强化原始残差反馈，而基于验证的校正强度选择限制了残差过度注入。在真实世界基准上以iTransformer为主冻结预测器，与多个强基线对比并测试跨骨干网络的迁移性，结果显示RATL在多数设置中进一步提升了基础预测器性能。

Conclusion: RATL将检索对象从历史目标值转移到特定于基础模型的历史预测误差，提供了一种即插式的、基于残差记忆的连续输出预测反馈校正范式，可有效提升冻结基础预测器在多数场景下的性能。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) complements parametric models with retrieved external evidence. The same idea is attractive for continuous-output regression, but directly reusing retrieved target values is often not robust when samples differ in output level, numerical scale, or local dynamics. Moreover, conventional forecasting pipelines generally use residuals for model optimization and error diagnosis, but do not retain individual historical residual examples as memory that can be accessed at inference time.For multivariate time-series forecasting, we propose RATL, a plug-in residual-retrieval and feedback-correction method. RATL freezes a base forecaster to construct retrieval keys and turns its historical forecast residuals into a train-only memory specific to that base model. At inference time, RATL retrieves residual trajectories from similar historical contexts subject to causal availability constraints, then uses a set-aware router operating over forecast blocks and variables to select and combine these trajectories. Experiments show that historical residuals matched to the current context contain reusable forecasting information and that RATL improves frozen base forecasters in most experimental settings. Ablations further show that learned routing strengthens raw residual feedback, while validation-based correction-strength selection limits residual over-injection.On real-world benchmarks, we use iTransformer as the primary frozen base forecaster, compare against multiple strong forecasting baselines, and test transferability across backbones. The results show that RATL can further improve base-forecaster performance in most settings.Overall, RATL shifts the retrieved object from historical target values to base-model-specific historical forecast errors, providing a plug-in, residual-memory-based paradigm for learned feedback correction in continuous-output forecasting.

</details>


### [319] [Headroom-Drift Replay: A Primitive for Principled Replay Control in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03941)
*Hyun Bin Park,Du-Seong Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Headroom-Drift Replay，一种面向 GRPO 的组级重放控制原语，将重放决策拆分为 Headroom（按剩余学习价值排序存储的组）与 Drift（按与当前策略兼容性门控）两个独立步骤，在不增加辅助生成或训练机制、不改变fresh on-policy流的前提下，在数学推理、多模态推理和 Agentic Search 基准上超越朴素重放并持平或超越更复杂的重放方法，同时在环境交互主导成本的 Agentic Search 中实现同等质量下的更低墙钟时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于RL的后训练中，重复的fresh rollout生成日益成为瓶颈，尤其在环境交互主导墙钟成本的智能体（agentic）场景中。重放能通过复用历史轨迹缓解该负担，但现有方法通常将其嵌入包含探索、经验重构或混合策略优化的更大训练管线中，导致重放本身的贡献难以被单独评估。作者希望聚焦回答一个明确问题：仅依靠有原则的重放选择能达到多大效果。

Method: 引入 Headroom-Drift Replay，一种用于 GRPO 的组级重放控制原语，将复用拆分为两个决策：Headroom 依据剩余学习价值对存储的组进行排序以决定重放优先级，Drift 依据与当前策略的兼容性对候选组进行门控筛选。fresh 的on-policy流保持不变，方法不增加任何辅助生成或训练机制，实现为轻量级单一干预。

Result: 在数学推理、多模态推理和 Agentic Search 基准上，该单一干预在 Avg Mean@32 指标上优于朴素重放，并与更广泛的重放方法持平或超过。在环境交互主导成本的 Agentic Search 中，该方法以更低墙钟时间获得可比质量。

Conclusion: 有原则的重放选择本身即可带来显著收益：仅通过对重放组进行排序（Headroom）与门控（Drift）两项轻量决策而不改动fresh on-policy流、不增加辅助训练机制，就足以超越朴素重放并在质量上持平或超过更复杂的重放管线，同时在交互密集的Agentic场景中显著节省墙钟成本，表明了可隔离、可解释的重放原语的价值。

Abstract: RL-based post-training for reasoning models is increasingly bottlenecked by repeated fresh rollout generation, particularly in agentic settings where environment interaction dominates wall-clock cost. Replay can reduce this burden by reusing past trajectories, but existing methods typically embed it within larger training pipelines involving exploration, experience restructuring, or mixed-policy optimization. This makes replay's own contribution difficult to isolate. We ask a focused question: how far can principled replay selection alone go? We introduce Headroom-Drift Replay, a group-level replay control primitive for GRPO that separates reuse into two decisions. Headroom ranks stored groups by remaining learning value, while Drift gates them by compatibility with the current policy. The fresh on-policy stream remains unchanged, and the method adds no auxiliary generation or training machinery. Across mathematical reasoning, multimodal reasoning, and Agentic Search benchmarks, this single intervention outperforms naive replay and matches or exceeds broader replay methods on Avg Mean@32. In Agentic Search, where environment interaction dominates cost, it delivers comparable quality at materially lower wall-clock time.

</details>


### [320] [VestigeKV: The NoPE-MLA KV Cache Carries Its Own Eviction Signal in a Vestigial Branch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03949)
*WenJie Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VestigeKV提出一种面向NoPE MLA模型（如Kimi Linear）的KV缓存压缩方法：利用缓存中本就携带的64维解耦分支（RoPE遗迹被NoPE训练重利用为显著性通道）作为查询无关的驱逐信号，仅读取每行11%，将缓存划分为“被关注层”与GPU常驻归档层（精确移动而非删除），实现无训练、无量化、无权重/内核修改的多倍率压缩。在8k-65k上下文下8x压缩检索保持1.00、32x保持0.92，零性能差距；而RoPE模型上该算子崩溃至0.08，证明查询无关显著性仅在无旋转时存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长生命周期的KV缓存必须在读取它的查询出现之前完成压缩，因此依赖观测注意力（如H2O、SnapKV）的选择方法在此失效——token的重要性尚未被观测到，在NoPE MLA模型上检索性能崩至0.00-0.33。作者旨在找到一种查询无关的驱逐信号，使压缩不依赖未来查询。

Method: 在Kimi Linear上，VestigeKV利用缓存自身携带的64维解耦分支作为查询无关的显著性信号（NoPE训练将该RoPE遗迹重利用为显著性通道）。读取每行的11%，将缓存划分为两层：top-m行保留在被关注层，其余行精确移动（而非删除）到GPU常驻归档层，并通过已认证触发器每步可达。该层在32x下仅0.25 KB（对比每token 8.1 KB缓存）。全程无训练、无量化、无权重或内核改动。

Result: 开销可忽略：在8k-65k上下文下8x压缩检索保持1.00、32x保持0.92，与全行选择零差距。归档层保持位精确且GPU常驻（以主机卸载作为回收VRAM的变体）。标准配置下的召回层在128x仍保持1.00。在RoPE MLA模型上，相同算子崩溃至0.08（普通驱逐为0.42）。top-1目标覆盖2.3-6.7% token（对比RoPE的10.2-46.8%），证明查询无关显著性仅在不旋转时存在，且RoPE下查询通用精确合并被证不可行。

Conclusion: VestigeKV通过“缓存自身携带的查询无关信号”实现了无需未来查询的KV缓存压缩，在NoPE MLA模型上取得无损级检索性能且零额外成本。该方法严格局限于NoPE模型（Kimi K3使用NoPE Gated-MLA变体，若缓存布局匹配则可能扩展，但作者不越界声称），且以阈值冻结在数据前的严谨方式（附20份存档判定与8条关闭路线）保障了结果的可信性。

Abstract: The problem. A long-lived KV cache must be compressed before the queries that will read it exist; selection by observed attention (H2O, SnapKV) collapses there (0.00-0.33 needle retrieval on a NoPE MLA model), because a token's importance has not yet been observed. The method. On Kimi Linear, VestigeKV evicts by a query-independent signal the cache already carries: the 64-dimensional decoupled branch, a vestige of RoPE that NoPE training repurposes into a salience channel. Reading 11% of each row, it partitions the cache: the top-m rows stay in the attended tier; every other row moves -- exactly, never deleted -- to a GPU-resident archive reachable per step by a certified trigger. No training, no quantization, no weight or kernel change. Cost. Nothing measurable: retrieval holds at 1.00 under 8x and 0.92 under 32x from 8k to 65k context, zero gap to full-row selection. The attended tier is 0.25 KB of Kimi Linear's 8.1 KB per-token cache at 32x; the archive stays bit-exact and GPU-resident, with host offload as the VRAM-reclaiming variant. The recall tier -- the standard configuration -- holds 128x at 1.00. Kimi K3 is reported to use a NoPE Gated-MLA variant; if its cache layout matches, the method plausibly extends there -- we make no claim beyond the measured model. NoPE exclusivity. The identical operator on a RoPE MLA collapses to 0.08 (plain eviction: 0.42); query-independent salience itself exists only without rotation (top-1 targets span 2.3-6.7% of tokens vs. 10.2-46.8%), and query-universal exact merging is provably impossible under RoPE. All thresholds were frozen before data; 20 archived verdicts and 8 closed routes accompany the paper.

</details>


### [321] [OSR: Output Space Redistribution for Adaptive Label Removal in Classification Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03972)
*Minyi Peng,Darian Gunamardi,Ivan Tjuawinata,Yongsen Zheng,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于输出空间统计重分布的模块化输出过滤器，用于近似重训练模型移除类别后的置信度向量，避免依赖原始数据和特征空间调整，兼顾效率与隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类系统随标签体系动态更新时需频繁移除类别，现有基于重训练或特征空间调整的方法存在依赖原始数据、计算与存储开销大、结果不一致、可扩展性差及模型效用下降等局限。

Method: 利用输出空间中的统计重分布来近似重训练模型移除类别后的置信度向量，作为模块化输出过滤器使用，无需特征空间调整或损失函数收敛，仅需现有标签和先前输出置信度。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在多个分类任务上能够与完全重训练的性能竞争，同时在计算效率和隐私保护方面有所提升。

Conclusion: 该方法有效缓解了标签移除场景下现有方法的局限，提供了一种高效、可扩展且隐私友好的替代方案。

Abstract: Label removal occurs frequently in classification systems with evolving taxonomies, where categories must be dynamically updated or eliminated. To accommodate such changes, classification models must adapt accordingly. Existing solutions, broadly categorized as retraining-based and feature-space-adjustment-based, share common limitations despite their variations, including reliance on access to original data, substantial computational and storage costs, inconsistent results, poor scalability, and degradation of model utility. To address this, we propose a novel approach that leverages statistical redistribution in the output space to approximate the post-removal confidence vectors of a retrained model. Applicable as a modular output filter, our method bypasses the burden of feature-space adjustments or loss-function convergence, alleviating scalability limitations. Furthermore, by requiring only existing labels and prior output confidences, the method potentially mitigates privacy concerns inherent to data-dependent solutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive performance against full retraining, with improvements in computational efficiency and privacy preservation across several classification tasks.

</details>


### [322] [RobustSeiz: An Open-Source Framework for Benchmarking the Robustness of EEG Seizure Detection Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04007)
*Mohammad Mohammadi,Alireza Zarei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RobustSeiz 是一个开源、模型无关的框架，用于在部署前对癫痫发作检测器进行标准化的压力测试和对比。它将四个公开头皮 EEG 语料库标准化为 BIDS-EEG 格式，在受控的临床动机分布偏移（环境、噪声、对抗变换）下评估受试者独立的检测器，并报告样本级和事件级的多种指标，包括 Docker 化 GPU 流水线和实验注册机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癫痫发作检测器在留出 EEG 数据上表现良好，但在真实世界的采集变异性、伪影和对抗输入下可能失效。现有评估仅基于干净数据的准确率，缺乏标准化、可复现的协议来衡量部署前的鲁棒性，因此需要统一的基准测试框架。

Method: 将四个公开头皮 EEG 语料库（CHB-MIT、TUSZ、Siena、SeizeIT1）标准化为 BIDS-EEG 树结构，在留出分区上评估受试者独立检测器。对环境、噪声和对抗变换在预设超参数网格上进行扫描。实验通过 Dockerized GPU 流水线运行，配以实验注册表，并提供完整评估与研究子集两种模式，记录蒙特卡洛 dropout 预测一致性。

Result: 使用 TUSZ 上的当代检测器演示了框架在完整变换网格上的运行；AWGN 分析展示了扰动严重程度如何改变检测质量、发作起始时间和预测一致性。框架验证了其在真实临床压力因素下评估检测器鲁棒性的能力。

Conclusion: RobustSeiz 提供了一种共享的基准测试标准，用于评估癫痫检测器在真实临床压力因素下的鲁棒性，将部署前评估从单纯干净数据准确率扩展到更全面的鲁棒性考量。

Abstract: Despite strong performance on held-out electroencephalography (EEG) data, seizure detectors may fail under real-world acquisition variability, artifacts, and adversarial inputs. We introduce RobustSeiz, an open-source, model-agnostic framework that provides a standardized, reproducible protocol for stress-testing and comparing seizure detectors under controlled, clinically motivated distribution shifts before deployment. We standardize four public scalp-EEG corpora (CHB-MIT, TUSZ, Siena, and SeizeIT1) into BIDS-EEG trees and evaluate subject-independent detectors on held-out splits. Environment, noise, and adversarial transforms are swept over predefined hyperparameter grids. Each run reports sample- and event-level sensitivity, precision, F1, false positives per 24 h, Lead and Lag onset timing, and Monte Carlo dropout predictive agreement. RobustSeiz includes a Dockerized GPU pipeline, experiment registry, and full-evaluation and research-subset modes. We demonstrate the framework with a contemporary seizure detector on TUSZ across the complete implemented shift grid; an AWGN analysis illustrates how perturbation severity changes detection quality, onset timing, and predictive agreement. RobustSeiz provides a shared benchmarking standard for evaluating seizure-detector robustness under realistic clinical stressors, extending pre-deployment assessment beyond clean-data accuracy.

</details>


### [323] [Unlocking Lossless Speedups in LLMs via Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04010)
*Subham Sekhar Sahoo,Lingjie Chen,Khiem Pham,Jonathan Geuter,Chaitanya Dwivedi,Varad Pimpalkhute,Yash Akhauri,Alexander Moreno,Mikhail Yurochkin,Zhenting Wang,Mostafa Elhoushi,Nolan Dey,Shane Bergsma,Joel Hestness,John Thickstun,Eric Xing,Zhengzhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的模型类别"扩散增强LLM"（Uno），将自回归（AR）模型分布与扩散采样结合，通过轻量级扩散权重实现多token并行生成，同时保留标准NTP训练。引入Ψ-Spec采样器族实现无损加速和推理时扩展，无需单独草稿模型，在吞吐量和质量上均优于投机解码和纯扩散LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的成功高度依赖next-token预测，但其自回归（AR）结构需要逐token的串行生成，导致生成速度缓慢，成为推理瓶颈。现有加速方案各有缺陷：投机解码需要额外的草稿模型；扩散LLM（d-LLM）在加速时会牺牲底层AR模型的质量。因此需要一种既保留AR模型质量又能并行生成的高效方法。

Method: 将模型参数解耦为两组：AR权重（用标准NTP目标训练）和轻量级扩散权重（用于同时生成多个token）。通过简单的"扩散蒸馏"阶段学习扩散权重，对现有LLM训练流程开销可忽略。引入Ψ-Spec采样器族，支持固定上下文长度下的无损加速与推理时扩展。模型Uno可从零训练，也可通过增强现有开源权重AR LLM构建。

Result: Uno在每一个评测批大小下吞吐量均高于领先的投机解码方法，相比基础AR模型最高可实现3倍加速（包括设备支持的最大批大小）。8B Uno模型在所有评测基准（agentic工具使用、编码、长上下文推理）上均优于领先的开源26B DiffusionGemma和专有Mercury 2模型。

Conclusion: 扩散增强方法在不牺牲AR模型质量的前提下实现了显著的并行生成加速，并且兼具可扩展性与通用性（可增强现有开源模型），在性能与速度上全面超越现有投机解码和纯扩散LLM方法，是一种高效的LLM加速生成新范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) owe much of their success to next-token prediction (NTP), but their autoregressive (AR) structure requires slow, sequential token generation. To overcome this bottleneck, we introduce diffusion-augmented LLMs, a new class of models that defines an AR model distribution while using diffusion to draw multiple tokens in parallel from that distribution. We decouple the parameters of these models into two sets: AR weights, trained using the standard NTP objective, and lightweight diffusion weights, trained to generate multiple tokens simultaneously. The diffusion weights are learned through a simple Diffusion Distillation phase that adds negligible overhead to existing LLM training pipelines. We also introduce $Ψ$-Spec, a family of samplers that enables lossless acceleration and inference-time scaling at a fixed context length. Unlike speculative decoding, our method requires no separate draft model. Unlike diffusion LLMs (d-LLMs), it accelerates generation without sacrificing the quality of the underlying AR model. The resulting models, called Uno, can be trained from scratch or built by augmenting existing open-weight AR LLMs. Uno achieves higher throughput than leading speculative-decoding methods at every evaluated batch size and delivers up to $3\times$ speedups over the base AR model, including at the largest batch size supported by the device. Notably, our 8B Uno model outperforms the leading open d-LLM, the 26B DiffusionGemma, and the proprietary Mercury 2 across all evaluated benchmarks in agentic tool use, coding, and long-context reasoning. We release code and checkpoints at: https://s-sahoo.github.io/uno/

</details>


### [324] [A location-invariant estimator of extremal quantile treatment effects for heavy-tailed distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04018)
*Xin Yu,Shuwei Huang,Jicheng Liu,Jielin Tang,Bolin Wang,Yunxiao Zhang,Tian Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对重尾潜在结果分布下极端分位数处理效应（QTE）的估计问题，指出现有极值QTE估计量在潜在结果的共同位置平移下不具不变性。作者通过两步修正：将位置不变的Fraga极值指数（EVI）估计量适配到因果设定（利用逆倾向得分加权），并用基于差分的外推方案替代原始外推公式使位置参数在分位数差分中被消去，最终得到一个位置不变的极值QTE估计量，并建立了其相合性与渐近正态性，给出了相合的方差估计量以实现渐近有效的推断。模拟研究验证了位置不变性、对阈值的稳定性及覆盖概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在重尾分布的应用场景中（如金融、保险等领域），目标分位数往往远超出数据范围，需要通过外推结合因果极值指数（EVI）估计量来估计极端分位数处理效应。然而，现有极值QTE估计量在潜在结果分布发生共同位置平移时不再保持不变，尽管总体QTE本身具有该不变性。这种不一致性会导致估计结果受测量基准或位置参数影响，从而破坏估计的可靠性与理论一致性，因此亟需修正。

Method: 方法分两步：第一步，将具有位置不变性的Fraga EVI估计量通过逆倾向得分加权（IPSW）适配到因果推断框架中，以获得位置不变的因果EVI估计；第二步，用基于差分的估计方案替代原始的外推公式，使得在计算分位数差分时位置参数相互抵消。此外，作者证明了所提极值QTE估计量的相合性与渐近正态性，并构造了相合的方差估计量以支持渐近有效的推断。

Result: 理论结果显示：所提的基于差分外推与位置不变因果EVI的QTE估计量具有位置不变性，且建立了相合性与渐近正态性，并提供了相合的方差估计量，从而支持渐近有效的统计推断。模拟研究表明该方法具备位置不变性、对阈值选择的稳定性，并达到预期的覆盖概率。

Conclusion: 作者成功解决了极端分位数处理效应估计在位置平移下不变性的理论缺陷：通过引入位置不变的因果Fraga EVI估计量与差分式外推方案，构造出的QTE估计量兼具位置不变性、良好的理论性质（相合性与渐近正态性）及可靠的推断表现，模拟结果也验证了其在实际中的稳健性。

Abstract: Quantile treatment effects (QTEs) measure the effect of a treatment on the distribution of an outcome, and their estimation at extreme quantile levels is of central interest in applications where the target quantiles lie far beyond the range of the data. For heavy-tailed potential outcomes, existing extremal QTE estimators rely on extrapolation combined with a causal extreme value index (EVI) estimator, but the resulting estimator is not invariant under a common location shift of the potential outcome distributions, even though the population QTE is. We address this issue in two steps. First, we adapt the location-invariant Fraga estimator of the EVI to the causal setting using inverse propensity score weighting. Second, we replace the original extrapolation formula with a difference-based scheme, under which the location parameter cancels when quantile differences are taken. The resulting QTE estimator is therefore location invariant. We establish the consistency and asymptotic normality of the proposed extremal QTE estimators, and provide a consistent variance estimator, leading to asymptotically valid inference. A simulation study confirms the location invariance, the stability with respect to the threshold, and the coverage of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [325] [Subspace Inference Enables Efficient Active Reward Learning from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04066)
*Yutai Zhou,Erdem Bıyık*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PreferenceEKF，将主动偏好学习建模为顺序贝叶斯滤波问题，通过扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）在低维参数子空间中追踪奖励模型不确定性，实现高效的不确定性估计与查询合成，在 D4RL 与 V-D4RL 基准上相较其他贝叶斯深度学习方法在样本效率、运行时间、可扩展性和校准方面更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF 虽强大但样本效率低下，主动学习是合成高质量偏好查询的关键；然而对大型神经网络奖励模型进行有效的不确定性量化仍是核心挑战。

Method: 将主动偏好学习视为顺序贝叶斯滤波问题，避开对完整神经网络参数空间的后验推断，而是在低维参数子空间内通过扩展卡尔曼滤波进行顺序推断，随偏好查询到达持续更新奖励模型后验，从而实现对神经网络参数的可扩展采样以高效计算主动学习的采集函数。

Result: 在 D4RL 与 V-D4RL 基准上，该方法相比其他贝叶斯深度学习方法具有更好的样本效率、运行时间、可扩展性与校准性，且习得的奖励模型驱动的离线强化学习策略性能具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 结果表明可扩展贝叶斯方法在 RLHF 的基于偏好的奖励建模中具有潜力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a powerful yet sample-inefficient approach for learning reward models from human preferences, making active learning a critical component in synthesizing informative preference queries. However, effective uncertainty quantification required for active learning remains a key challenge for large neural network reward models. In this paper, we introduce PreferenceEKF, a sample-efficient approach that tracks reward model uncertainty by framing active preference learning as a sequential Bayesian filtering problem. Instead of relying on computationally prohibitive posterior inference over the full neural network parameter space, our method performs sequential inference via an extended Kalman filter within a low-dimensional parameter subspace, continuously updating the reward model posterior as new preference queries arrive. Our approach enables scalable sampling of neural network parameters to efficiently compute acquisition functions for active reward learning. Experiments on the D4RL and V-D4RL benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves better sample efficiency, runtime, scalability, and calibration compared to other Bayesian deep learning approaches, and the learned reward models lead to competitive offline reinforcement learning policy performance. This highlights the potential of scalable Bayesian methods for preference-based reward modeling in RLHF. Our code is available at https://github.com/yutaizhou/bnn_pref.

</details>


### [326] [Conditioning Degenerate Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04090)
*Uğur Aydın,Tamer Başar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对奇异扩散系数且底层（条件）密度不存在或不光滑的条件生成模型，利用因果最优输运定义近似损失函数，在最小假设下识别用于引导的最小熵控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件生成模型在训练中严重依赖得分函数进行引导，但当扩散系数奇异且密度不存在或不光滑时，传统方法失效，需要更鲁棒的理论框架。

Method: 借助因果最优输运及其通过（条件）扩散过程的可预测表示性质的表征（à la Üstünel），在相关鞅问题适定的条件下定义近似损失函数。

Result: 在最小假设下成功识别出用于引导的最小熵控制，使得条件生成模型在奇异扩散及无光滑密度场景下仍可训练。

Conclusion: 因果最优输运为奇异扩散条件生成提供理论基础，拓展了传统得分函数引导方法的适用边界。

Abstract: Current conditioned generative models heavily rely on score functions for guidance during training. When the generative model is a diffusion process with a singular diffusion coefficient and the underlying (conditional) densities either do not exist or are not smooth, we use causal optimal transport to define \emph{approximate} loss functions that identify a minimum-entropy control for guidance under minimal assumptions. Our approach relies on causal optimal transport and its characterization through the predictable representation property of (conditioned) diffusion processes whose associated martingale problem is well posed, à la Üstünel.

</details>


### [327] [Hardware-Aware FP4 FlashAttention-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04105)
*Robert Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Direct-P方案，在Blackwell 4位浮点（FP4）张量核心上解决attention因softmax转换和片上依赖而无法自动加速的问题。Direct-P将分数直接映射为FP4概率，在非因果推理中使前向吞吐最高达BF16的2.13倍（NVIDIA GB200）；因果路径利用保存的量化查询/键重建概率，并使用FP8梯度操作数，使单卡80亿参数完整更新加速最高1.14倍；匹配的分布式训练保留FP8概率和值，而所有MXFP4概率/值训练轨迹均发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blackwell的FP4张量核心虽然能加速矩阵乘法，但当矩阵乘积缩小时，softmax转换和片上依赖成为瓶颈，导致attention并未自动变快，因此需要专门设计来降低这些开销。

Method: 提出Direct-P：非因果推理路径将分数直接映射为FP4概率；因果路径将前向量化直接传入反向，通过保存的量化查询与键重建概率，并使用FP8梯度操作数；分布式训练中保留FP8概率与值。

Result: 在NVIDIA GB200上，Direct-P的非因果前向吞吐最高达BF16的2.13倍；单GPU 80亿参数训练的完整更新最高加速1.14倍；所有MXFP4概率/值训练轨迹均发散，而匹配的分布式训练保持稳定。

Conclusion: Direct-P有效克服了FP4张量核心下attention的软化与依赖瓶颈，实现了前向与完整训练更新的可测量加速，并表明保留FP8概率/值对于训练稳定性至关重要。

Abstract: Blackwell's 4-bit floating-point (FP4) tensor cores do not automatically make attention faster because softmax conversion and on-chip dependencies dominate once its matrix products shrink. We address this with \emph{Direct-P} for noncausal inference and a causal path that passes the forward quantization directly into backward. Direct-P maps scores directly to FP4 probabilities and reaches up to 2.13$\times$ the bfloat16 (BF16) forward throughput on an NVIDIA GB200. The causal path reconstructs probabilities from saved quantized queries and keys and uses 8-bit floating-point (FP8) gradient operands, accelerating a complete single-GPU 8-billion-parameter update by up to 1.14$\times$. Matched distributed training retains FP8 probabilities and values; every tested MXFP4 probability/value training trajectory diverges.

</details>


### [328] [Constant regret in general games via higher-order optimism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04113)
*Omar Abbadi,Rida Laraki,Panayotis Mertikopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非耦合学习算法——高阶乐观折扣（HOOD），当所有玩家在任意N人标准式博弈（每人最多K个动作）中使用时，能保证O(N³log²K)的个人遗憾，且在博弈时间范围内均匀成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一般博弈中实现常数遗憾（constant regret）一直是未解决的难题，此前方法的主要障碍在于诱导博弈序列会产生剧烈振荡。作者旨在通过精心设计的算法消除这一障碍，从而在一般N人博弈中实现较低的个人遗憾界。

Method: HOOD是乐观跟随正则化领导者（OptFTRL）的变体，其核心是将折扣的(N+1)阶预测器与在博弈策略空间适当"提升"（lifting）后的熵正则化相结合。这种组合设计旨在以可控方式抑制诱导博弈序列的大幅振荡。

Result: 该算法在任意N人标准式博弈中实现了O(N³log²K)的个人遗憾界，该界在整个博弈时间范围内均匀成立，显著优于此前的工作。

Conclusion: 本文通过组合高阶乐观预测器、折扣机制与策略空间的提升，成功攻克了此前在一般博弈中实现常数遗憾的关键难点。该工作与Liu、Farina和Ozdaglar的独立研究（O(N²¹log⁴K)遗憾界）存在显著相似之处，但本文在遗憾界上更为高效。

Abstract: We introduce an uncoupled learning algorithm which, when employed by all players of an arbitrary $N$-player normal form game with up to $K$ actions per player, guarantees $O(N^3\log^2 K)$ individual regret, uniformly over the horizon of play. The proposed algorithm - which we call higher-order optimism with discounting (HOOD) is a variant of optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader (OptFTRL) that combines a discounted $(N+1)$-th order predictor with entropic regularization over a suitable "lifting" of the game's strategy space. This combination of ingredients is purposefully designed to dampen large oscillations of the induced sequence of play in a controlled manner, removing in this way a key stumbling block of previous attempts to achieve constant regret in general games. Our approach bears several striking similarities to the concurrent - and completely independent - work of Liu, Farina, and Ozdaglar (arXiv:2608.31166), who very recently derived an $O(N^{21}\log^{4} K)$ regret bound through the use of higher-order optimism and an exponential moving average estimator.

</details>


### [329] [Prospective Coding Improves Learning in Deep Continuous-Time Recurrent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04134)
*Shivang Rawat,Mirko Morello,Flaviano Morone,David J. Heeger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归正交滤波器（RQFs），一种受生物学启发的复值时间滤波器（对角状态空间模型的特例），并通过前瞻性输入编码（prospective-input coding）修正深度连续时间递归网络中自底向上信号延迟与自顶向下误差衰减的问题。前瞻性变体在所有模型和配置下均匹配或超越非前瞻性对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间积分赋予连续时间递归网络记忆能力，但在深层堆叠中会延迟自底向上信号并衰减自顶向下误差。作者希望探究能否通过使每一层的自底向上输入具有前瞻性（prospective）来缓解这一失效模式。

Method: 从能量模型出发推导RQF动力学，证明每个RQF是带通滤波器，其可学习参数控制调谐频率和带宽。随后用无参数的二抽头更新使每层自底向上输入前瞻化，该更新不改变递归转移和并行扫描，并可推广到一般对角SSM，缓解时间梯度截断（仅空间反向传播）时的深度依赖性梯度衰减。

Result: 在RQF、S5和ORGaNICs上分别用完整BPTT与仅空间反向传播训练，前瞻性变体在所有模型和配置下匹配或超越非前瞻性对照。非残差width-32六层RQF在raw-audio Speech Commands上以31.9k参数达96.09%准确率；width-64六层RQF在16,384步Path-X任务上达83.56%。

Conclusion: RQFs被确认为参数高效的递归基底，前瞻性输入编码是深度连续时间递归网络的一种输入侧校正机制。

Abstract: Temporal integration gives continuous-time recurrent networks memory, but in deep stacks it also delays bottom-up signals and attenuates top-down errors. We develop Recursive Quadrature Filters (RQFs), biologically motivated complex-valued temporal filters that are a special case of diagonal state-space models (SSMs), and ask whether this failure mode can be addressed by making each layer's bottom-up input prospective. Starting from an energy model, we derive the RQF dynamics and show that each RQF is a band-pass filter whose learnable parameters control its tuning frequency and bandwidth. We then make each layer's bottom-up input prospective using a parameter-free two-tap update that leaves the recurrent transition and parallel scan unchanged. We extend this correction to general diagonal SSMs and show that it mitigates depth-dependent gradient attenuation when temporal gradients are truncated, i.e., spatial-only backpropagation. We evaluate the intervention in RQFs, S5, and ORGaNICs (a nonlinear gated RNN) trained using full backpropagation through time (BPTT) and spatial-only backpropagation. Under full BPTT, prospective variants match or outperform their non-prospective controls in every model and configuration. A non-residual width-32 six-layer RQF reaches 96.09% accuracy on raw-audio Speech Commands with 31.9k parameters; a width-64 six-layer RQF reaches 83.56% on the 16,384-step Path-X task. These results identify RQFs as a parameter-efficient recurrent substrate and prospective-input coding as an input-side correction for deep continuous-time recurrent networks.

</details>


### [330] [A Low-Cost, Open Platform for End-to-End Autonomous Driving on a Miniature Ackermann Vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04147)
*Gustavo Claudio Karl Couto,Eric Aislan Antonelo,Gabriel George Zipperer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个低成本、开源的微型Ackermann车辆端到端自动驾驶实验平台，集成实体车辆、印刷城市赛道、数据采集工具、轨迹配准和Webots数字孪生，支持仿真到现实的可控实验。作为首个基线，实现了命令条件行为克隆，实体车闭环实验中平均横向误差6.1cm（人类演示为4.7cm）；数字孪生中相机视场角从58°扩至120°可使误差从35.6cm降至3.3cm。通过数字孪生合成数据与sim-to-real图像翻译器结合，高容量策略成为唯一能完成全部四条赛道闭环任务的配置，证明该平台是实用的sim-to-real测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶研究长期受限于昂贵的实体平台和仿真与真实世界之间的差距（sim-to-real gap）。作者旨在提供一个低成本、可复现、开源的实验平台，将仿真方法与实体执行连接起来，以支持可控、可重复的自动驾驶研究，并弥合目前该领域缺乏标准化低成本测试平台的问题。

Method: 平台由五个组件构成：实体微型Ackermann车辆、印刷城市赛道、数据采集工具、轨迹配准模块和Webots数字孪生。算法基线采用命令条件行为克隆——神经网络策略接收车载相机图像和高层导航命令，直接输出转向和速度。方法包括真实数据与合成数据训练、学习的sim-to-real图像翻译器以缩小外观差异，并对比不同容量网络的性能。

Result: 实体车闭环实验中，学习的策略能跟随车道并执行转弯，平均横向误差6.1cm，接近人类演示的4.7cm。数字孪生中相机视场角对性能影响显著：从58°扩至120°使横向误差从35.6cm降至3.3cm。结合合成数据与真实演示训练的高容量策略是唯一能闭环完成全部四条赛道配置的方案，而紧凑基线网络及仅用真实数据训练的同款网络完成的赛道较少。

Conclusion: 该开源平台被验证为实用的sim-to-real研究测试平台，为命令条件模仿学习提供了初始基线，能够将仿真中的自动驾驶方法可靠地转移到实体执行。作者将发布该平台以支持可复现研究，推动低成本、可复现的自动驾驶研究范式。

Abstract: This paper presents a low-cost, open experimental platform for research in end-to-end autonomous driving with miniature Ackermann vehicles. The platform combines a physical vehicle, a printed urban track, data collection tools, trajectory registration, and a Webots digital twin, enabling controlled experiments that connect simulation-based autonomous-driving methods to real-world execution. As a first baseline, we implement command-conditioned behavior cloning, in which a neural policy receives an on-board camera image and a high-level navigation command and outputs steering and speed. The system is evaluated both on the physical vehicle and in simulation. In real closed-loop experiments, the learned policy follows lanes and executes commanded turns, reaching a mean cross-track error of 6.1 cm with respect to the reference route, close to the 4.7 cm observed in human demonstrations. In the digital twin, camera field of view has a strong effect on performance, reducing the mean cross-track error from 35.6 to 3.3 cm when widened from 58 to 120 degrees. Using the digital twin to generate synthetic driving data and a learned sim-to-real image translator to reduce the appearance gap, we further show that a higher-capacity policy trained on this synthetic data combined with real demonstrations is the only configuration that completes all four track routes in closed loop, whereas the compact baseline and the same network trained on real data alone complete fewer. These results establish the open platform as a practical testbed for sim-to-real studies and provide an initial command-conditioned imitation-learning baseline; we release it to support reproducible research.

</details>


### [331] [Robust PAC Learning of Concurrent Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04189)
*Angel Y. He,David Parker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了面向一般和并发随机博弈（CSGs）的PAC学习框架，在转移不确定性下同时解决纳什均衡（NE）存在性这一难题。算法基于数据驱动的L1置信集求解鲁棒CSG，得到社会福利最优的ε-NE，并通过鲁棒MDP探索机制驱动联合状态-动作覆盖。关键创新是"纳什余量"（Nash margin）刻画，使框架要么返回社会福利值ε接近最优的ε近似NE，要么提供"不存在精确NE"的可靠证明。在最小可达性条件p_reach>0下，算法以多项式轨迹样本数终止，样本复杂度为O(R_max^2 H^4 |S|^2 |A| / (p_reach ε^2))。基准CSG实验验证了近乎最优性能、对NE（不）存在性的正确处理以及与理论一致的样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般和并发随机博弈（CSGs）在交互式决策与博弈论中具有广泛的现实应用，但带有转移不确定性的CSG学习问题此前缺乏PAC级理论保证。更为棘手的挑战在于，一般和博弈中纳什均衡（NE）的存在性并不总是有保证的，而现有基于学习的求解方法往往盲目假设NE存在。因此，作者希望构建一个首个具备PAC样本复杂度保证的CSG学习框架，并设计一种既能学习近似NE、又能严谨判定NE（不）存在性的机制，填补理论与算法上的空白。

Method: 算法采用"探索-估计-鲁棒求解"的循环结构：维护基于采集轨迹构建的数据驱动L1置信集以包裹真实转移核；在每个回合求解一个鲁棒CSG（在最坏情况转移核下）来生成社会福利最优的ε-NE；采用鲁棒MDP驱动的探索机制来系统增加联合状态-动作对的覆盖。核心理论创新是引入了"纳什余量"（Nash margin）刻画，用以量化均衡相对可能转移的最坏情况余量——据此，框架要么同时保证返回的ε-NE在转移不确定性下社会福利值ε接近最优，要么当该余量为负时给出严格证书证明不存在精确NE。在最弱可达性条件p_reach>0下，算法在多项式样本数内终止。

Result: 算法在最小可达性条件p_reach>0下以多项式数量的轨迹样本终止，获得样本复杂度O(R_max^2 H^4 |S|^2 |A| / (p_reach ε^2))。实证方面，在基准CSG上，算法表现出近乎最优的性能，能够正确处理NE的（不）存在性判断，且实测样本复杂度与理论界一致。该结果为CSG在转移不确定性下的PAC学习与均衡存在性验证提供了首个统一框架。

Conclusion: 作者得出的核心结论是：通过纳什余量刻画，可以在一个PAC学习框架内同时实现两个目标——在NE存在时学到社会福利ε最优的ε近似NE，在NE不存在时提供可靠的形式化证据。这为一般和并发随机博弈的样本高效学习与均衡存在性判定奠定了理论基础，并得到实验数据的支持，为后续研究（如放松可达性假设、改进探索策略）指明了方向。

Abstract: We introduce the first Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learning framework for general-sum concurrent stochastic games (CSGs) with transition uncertainty, while addressing the challenge of Nash equilibrium (NE) existence. Our algorithm maintains data-driven $L^1$ confidence sets over transition kernels and solves a robust CSG to compute a social-welfare optimal $\varepsilon$-NE, using a robust MDP-based exploration mechanism to drive joint state-action coverage. Crucially, we introduce a Nash margin characterisation that enables principled reasoning about equilibrium existence: the framework either returns an $\varepsilon$-approximate NE whose social-welfare value is $\varepsilon$-close to optimal, or provides a sound certificate that no exact NE exists. Under a minimum reachability condition $p_{\mathrm{reach}}>0$ over relevant state-action pairs, the algorithm terminates after a polynomial number of trajectory samples, with sample complexity $\widetilde{O}\left( {R_{\max}^2 H^4 |S|^2 |A| / (p_{\mathrm{reach}} \varepsilon^2)} \right)$. Empirical results on benchmark CSGs demonstrate near-optimal performance, correct handling of equilibrium (non-)existence, and sample complexity consistent with theory.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [332] [CAPQ-FAST: Content-Adaptive Perceived Quality Assessment for Faster Audiovisual Playback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03498)
*Jiarun Song,Yuxin Song,Fuzheng Yang,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文针对快速播放场景下用户感知质量研究不足的问题，通过主观实验分析了视频、语音、音乐及音视频内容在不同播放速度下的感知质量，提取时间信息（TI）、语速（WPM）和节拍（BPM）等内容固有特征，分别构建视频、语音、音乐的质量预测模型，并整合为统一的内容自适应快速播放感知质量评估模型（CAPQ-FAST），实验表明该模型能有效预测不同播放速度下的感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速播放已成为现代在线音视频服务的常见功能，但不同内容模态（视频、音频、音视频）在快速播放下对可理解性和信息完整性的要求各不相同，导致感知质量存在明显差异。然而，用户在不同播放速度下的感知质量尚未得到充分研究，这一研究空白限制了服务提供商对用户观看意图和感知体验的理解。

Method: 首先开展一系列主观实验，分析播放速度与视频、音频、音视频内容感知质量之间的关系；然后提取内容固有特征以捕捉时间动态，包括视频的时间信息（TI）、语音的每分钟字数（WPM）和音乐的每分钟节拍数（BPM）；分别针对视频、语音和音乐开发感知质量预测模型，并将这些模型整合为统一的内容自适应感知质量评估模型（CAPQ-FAST）。

Result: 实验结果表明，CAPQ-FAST 模型能够有效预测不同播放速度下的感知质量，验证了基于内容固有特征（TI、WPM、BPM）构建的分模态预测及整合方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 该模型可帮助服务提供商更好地理解用户在快速播放下的观看意图和感知体验，从而实现更具适应性的个性化推荐和播放控制，增强用户体验的自适应性。

Abstract: Faster playback has become a common feature in modern online audiovisual services, allowing users to consume content in less time while still maintaining a coherent viewing experience. However, different modalities of media content, such as video, audio (including speech and music), and audiovisual, exhibit varying requirements for understandability and information integrity under faster playback. These differences lead to noticeable variations in perceived quality depending on the content type. Nevertheless, users' perceived quality at different playback speeds remains insufficiently investigated. To address this gap, this paper conducts a series of subjective experiments to analyze the relationship between playback speed and perceived quality for video, audio, and audiovisual content. Content-specific intrinsic features are extracted to capture temporal dynamics, including temporal information (TI) for video, words per minute (WPM) for speech, and beats per minute (BPM) for music. Predictive models of perceived quality under faster playback are then developed separately for video, speech, and music. By integrating these models, a unified content-adaptive perceived quality assessment model (CAPQ-FAST) is proposed for faster audiovisual playback. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed model effectively predicts perceived quality under different playback speeds. This model can help service providers better understand users' viewing intentions and perceptual experiences under faster playback, thereby enabling more adaptive personalized recommendations and playback control to enhance user experience adaptability.

</details>
